# Project Verification & Testing Plan

This document defines the verification procedures for all project requirements. This project follows a **Test-Driven Development (TDD)** approach, where tests are defined before implementation.

## 1. Automated Unit Tests (SWE.4)

*These tests verify the core logic of individual software units without requiring a physical device.*

| Test ID | Jira ID | Component | Requirement ID(s) | Test Description | Status |
|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|

| **TST-EXP-005** | `ATT-902`, `ATT-1105` | **Smart Asymmetric Strava Naming & Pre-Upload Discovery Test** | `REQ-EXP-008` | 1. Unit test pre-upload duplicate discovery (`findExistingStravaActivityForWorkout`): verify when Strava API returns an activity matching the workout's start time, TCX file upload (`POST /api/v3/uploads`) is bypassed, avoiding duplicate activity creation on Strava, and `doUpdate(isDuplicate = true)` is invoked.<br>2. Unit test pre-upload duplicate discovery when no matching activity exists: verify `findExistingStravaActivityForWorkout` returns null and normal TCX upload proceeds.<br>3. Unit test duplicate upload handling (`isDuplicate = true`) where Strava title is a default time-of-day name (e.g. `"Radfahrt am Morgen"`, `"Lauf am Abend ⛅"`, `"Morning Ride"`): verify Strava update request omits `name` (preserving Strava title) and local SQLite `WorkoutSummaries.WORKOUT_NAME` preserves local app workout name (not overwritten by default Strava title).<br>4. Unit test duplicate upload handling where Strava title is custom (e.g. `"Sunday Club Ride"`, `"Schwarzwald Tour"`): verify Strava update request omits `name` (preserving Strava title) and local SQLite `WorkoutSummaries.WORKOUT_NAME` is enriched with the custom Strava title.<br>5. Unit test `isDefaultStravaName()` regex evaluator across German and English time-of-day templates, with and without weather emojis (`⛅`, `🌧️`, `☀️`), and assert custom titles return `false`.<br>6. Unit test duplicate upload detection resilience: verify `checkAndUpdateDuplicate` identifies duplicate activity IDs when returned in `error` or `status` fields across varied phrasings (`"duplicate of activity <id>"`, `"duplicate of <id>"`).<br>7. Unit test fresh upload (`isDuplicate = false`) where workout name equals `fileBaseName`: verify Strava update request omits `name` parameter.<br>8. Unit test fresh upload where workout name is custom: verify Strava update request contains `name`.<br>9. Verify other metadata (`sportName`, `gearId`, `description`, `trainer`, `commute`) continue to be updated in both paths.<br>10. Unit test reactive in-memory cache update in `WorkoutRepository`: verify `reloadExportStatusesFor` refreshes `workoutName` in memory when updated in SQLite. | All test cases pass; existing Strava activities bypass TCX file upload, Strava titles are never overwritten, local app names are preserved when Strava title is default, custom Strava titles enrich local app database, and fresh uploads auto-name or serialize custom titles properly. | Verified |

| **TST-EXP-010** | `ATT-1190` / `ATT-1214`, `ATT-1215` | **Minimized Strava Activity Feedback Persistence & Backward-Compatible Rendering Verification** | `REQ-EXP-013` | 1. *Unit Test `StravaActivityParser.minimize` Data Stripping*:<br>• Provide a realistic raw Strava activity JSON containing athlete profile, kudos, comments, external map polyline URLs, photos, segment efforts, and best efforts.<br>• Verify the minimized JSON payload includes `"v": 2` and strictly preserves `id`, `segment_efforts`, and `best_efforts`.<br>• Verify all transient social fields (`kudos_count`, `comment_count`, `athlete`, `photos`, `map`) are 100% stripped.<br>2. *Unit Test Null & Default Value Omission*:<br>• Provide segment efforts and best efforts with null PR/KOM ranks and zero distances.<br>• Verify that null ranks and non-positive distances are omitted from the serialized JSON rather than serialized as explicit nulls.<br>3. *Unit Test Parser Backward Compatibility & UI Parity*:<br>• Parse both legacy full JSON (v1) and minimized JSON (v2) through `StravaActivityParser.parse()`.<br>• Verify returned `StravaActivity` models are identical in segment effort counts, PR ranks, starred flags, and best efforts.<br>• Verify `SegmentPrCelebrationBanner` renders identical celebration state.<br>4. *Unit Test Defensive Exception Handling & Truncation Resilience*:<br>• Feed truncated, malformed, and empty JSON strings to `StravaActivityParser.parse()`; verify it returns `null` safely without throwing runtime exceptions.<br>5. *Unit Test Strict CQS Invariance*:<br>• Verify `StravaUploadDbHelper.getStravaActivityData()` executes zero SQLite write operations or row updates.<br>6. *Unit Test SQLite Migration to DB_VERSION 6*:<br>• Initialize `StravaUploadDbHelper` at version 5 with legacy uncompacted JSON rows and malformed JSON rows.<br>• Trigger `onUpgrade` to version 6; verify legacy rows are migrated in-place to `"v": 2` schema, malformed rows safely become null/tombstone without halting migration, and unhandled disk errors roll back cleanly.<br>7. *Unit Test Disconnect Data Purge & WAL Checkpoint*:<br>• Verify `StravaDataPurgeManager.purgeAllStravaData()` clears `StravaUploads` table completely and triggers WAL checkpoint.<br>8. *Regression Suite*:<br>• Execute `./gradlew testDebugUnitTest` across all modules with zero regressions. | Data minimization achieves 90%+ storage reduction; transient social fields are completely eliminated; offline celebration and segment rendering retain 100% parity; CQS, DB_VERSION 6 migration, and disconnect wipe invariants are preserved. | Verified |

## 4. Release Verification Workflow

Whenever a file is modified, the following workflow is triggered:

1.  **Change Detection**: The agent identifies which files were touched.
2.  **Requirement Mapping**: The agent cross-references the files with `docs/requirements.md` (Implementation Column) to identify affected features.
3.  **Test Selection**: The agent selects the relevant **Verification ID** from the requirements table.
4.  **Co-Execution**: The user and agent perform the verification. The agent updates the `Status` column in `docs/requirements.md` upon success.

Verify method returns cleanly without error.<br>• Transaction serialization: Verify all database operations execute within SQLite transaction boundaries.<br>3. *TrackerService Workout Start Lifecycle Unit Tests (`TrackerServiceWorkoutStartTest.kt`)*:<br>• Normal workout start: Trigger `sampleAndWriteToDb()` with `mStartType == START_NORMAL` and valid GPS coordinates. abwählen" clears active navigation.<br>4. *Automated Route Detection Engine (`RouteAutoDetector`)*:<br>• Distance $\le 30\text{ m}$ for $\ge 200\text{ m}$ with heading alignment ($\Delta \theta \le | **TST-FIL-004** | `ATT-1621`, `ATT-1738` | **Stabilized VAM (Vertical Speed) Metric, Location Accuracy Gating & Indoor Drift Suppression Verification** | `REQ-FIL-012` | 1. *Stationary Indoor Drift Suppression Tests (`VerticalSpeedAndSlopeDeviceTest.kt`)*:<br>• Simulate stationary athlete (`speed = 0.0 m/s` or `speed = 0.2 m/s`) with altitude oscillating randomly by $\pm 0.5\text{ m}$ around 100m.<br>• Verify `mVerticalSpeedSensor` broadcasts `0 m/h` and does not emit phantom climbing rates.<br>2. *Location Accuracy Gating Verification (`VerticalSpeedAndSlopeDeviceTest.kt`)*:<br>• Simulate athlete with degraded GPS accuracy ($> 20.0\text{ m}$, e.g. 25m or 35m) indoors/basement while altitude fluctuates.<br>• Verify `calculateVam` suppresses vertical speed to `0 m/h` whenever GPS accuracy exceeds `VAM_MAX_GPS_ACCURACY_METERS = 20.0`.<br>3. *Low-Speed Indoor GPS Wander Suppression Tests (`VerticalSpeedAndSlopeDeviceTest.kt`)*:<br>• Simulate indoor wander with phantom horizontal speed between $0.5\text{ m/s}$ and $1.1\text{ m/s}$ ($2.0\dots 4.0\text{ km/h}$) and altitude jitter ($|\text{raw\_vam}| < 150\text{ m/h}$).<br>• Verify output is clamped to `0 m/h` by low-speed drift damping.<br>4. *Steady Climbing Accuracy & Convergence Tests (`VerticalSpeedAndSlopeDeviceTest.kt`)*:<br>• Simulate athlete climbing at steady 800 m/h ($0.222\text{ m/s}$) at 5 m/s horizontal speed with good accuracy ($5.0\text{ m}$).<br>• Verify calculated VAM smoothly converges to $800 \pm 15\text{ m/h}$ and maintains stability without second-by-second jitter exceeding $\pm 20\text{ m/h}$.<br>5. *Elevator / Lift Vertical Movement While Stationary Tests (`VerticalSpeedAndSlopeDeviceTest.kt`)*:<br>• Simulate athlete in an elevator with horizontal speed $0.0\text{ m/s}$ ascending at $2.0\text{ m/s}$ ($7200\text{ m/h}$, clamped to $3000\text{ m/h}$) with acceptable accuracy.<br>• Verify VAM is NOT suppressed to 0, but correctly registers vertical motion.<br>6. *Descent & Flat Road Deadband Tests (`VerticalSpeedAndSlopeDeviceTest.kt`)*:<br>• Simulate flat road riding ($speed = 8.0\text{ m/s}$) with minor barometric noise ($|\text{raw\_vam}| = 20\text{ m/h}$): verify output is `0 m/h`.<br>• Simulate steady descent at $-600\text{ m/h}$: verify output converges to $-600 \pm 15\text{ m/h}$.<br>7. *Single-Tick GPS Glitch Rejection Tests (`VerticalSpeedAndSlopeDeviceTest.kt`)*:<br>• Inject single-sample +30m step glitch into an otherwise steady altitude stream.<br>• Verify VAM does not explode into tens of thousands of m/h, and remains safely clamped within $[-3000, 3000]\text{ m/h}$.<br>8. *Clean-Room Full Suite Regression Execution*:<br>• Execute `./gradlew testDebugUnitTest` across all modules. | Vertical speed is smoothed via 15-second least-squares linear regression; location accuracy gating (>20m) and low-speed drift damping (<1.2 m/s) zero out indoor wander and phantom climbing; genuine vertical motion is preserved; noise deadband and outlier clamping enforced; 100% test pass rate. | Verified |

| **TST-STB-013** | `ATT-2309` | **Resilient Single-Pass Workout History Streaming & CursorWindow Crash Protection Verification** | `REQ-STB-013` | 1. *Single-Pass Sequential Streaming Unit Tests (`WorkoutRepositoryStreamTest.kt`)*:<br>• Verify that `loadAllWorkouts()` executes in a single forward pass without calling `c.moveToPosition(...)` or seeking backward across rows.<br>• Verify that chunks of 50 are extracted into `RawCursorSnapshot` and vectorized metadata queries (`extrema`, `stravaData`, `clusterNames`, `laps`) execute in batch.<br>• Verify that all loaded workouts are accurately mapped with their metadata and export statuses.<br>2. *CursorWindow Boundary & Synthetic 2,000 Workouts Stress Test (`WorkoutRepositoryStressTest.kt`)*:<br>• Simulate an extensive dataset of 2,000 synthetic workouts backed by a MockCursor.<br>• Verify that all 2,000 workouts stream completely to completion into `allWorkouts` without throwing `IllegalStateException`.<br>• Verify that progressive UI updates are emitted at expected thresholds (first 10, each batch of 50, and completion).<br>3. *Defensive Row Exception Shielding Verification*:<br>• Simulate a cursor row throwing `IllegalStateException` or `CursorWindowAllocationException` during row extraction.<br>• Verify that the repository catches the exception, logs a warning, skips the faulty row, and completes streaming the remaining rows without crashing the process.<br>4. *Clean-Room Full Suite Regression Execution*:<br>• Execute `./gradlew testDebugUnitTest` verifying 100% pass rate with zero regressions. | Workouts stream sequentially in a strictly monotonic forward-only pass, backward seeking across CursorWindow boundaries is completely eliminated, low-level row exceptions are shielded safely, and synthetic datasets of 2,000 items stream without `IllegalStateException`; 100% test pass rate. | Verified |
| **TST-STB-014** | `ATT-2661` | **TrainingApplication Foreground Service Startup Delegation & Exception Shielding Verification** | `REQ-STB-014` | 1. *Foreground Service Startup Delegation Unit Test (`TrainingApplicationTrackingTest.kt`)*:<br>• Verify that `startTracking()` delegates to `ContextCompat.startForegroundService()` instead of `startService()` when targeting `TrackerService`.<br>• Verify tracking mode transitions to `TrackingMode.TRACKING`.<br>2. *Background Launch Restriction Shielding Unit Test (`TrainingApplicationTrackingTest.kt`)*:<br>• Simulate `ContextCompat.startForegroundService()` throwing `IllegalStateException("Not allowed to start service ...: app is in background")`.<br>• Verify the exception is caught cleanly without terminating the application process.<br>• Verify tracking mode resets to `TrackingMode.READY`.<br>• Verify `TrackerService.showTrackingInterruptedNotification()` is displayed to provide user recovery.<br>3. *SecurityException Permission Shielding Unit Test (`TrainingApplicationTrackingTest.kt`)*:<br>• Simulate `ContextCompat.startForegroundService()` throwing `SecurityException("Missing FGS permission")`.<br>• Verify the exception is caught cleanly and logged, preventing crash.<br>4. *Clean-Room Full Suite Regression Execution*:<br>• Run `./gradlew testDebugUnitTest` verifying 100% pass rate with zero regressions. | `TrainingApplication.startTracking()` launches TrackerService via `startForegroundService()`; Android background launch restrictions (`IllegalStateException`) and permission errors (`SecurityException`) are shielded with clean recovery; 100% test pass rate. | Verified |
| **TST-UI-216** | `ATT-2059` | **Compact Equipment Card Layout & Harmonized Inter-Card Spacing Verification** | `REQ-UI-257` | 1. *Layout Metrics & Spacing Contract Test (`EquipmentCardSpacingContractTest.kt`)*:<br>• Verify `EquipmentList` configures `verticalArrangement` with compact spacing (6.dp–8.dp).<br>• Verify `MappableListItem` inside `EquipmentItem` has `vertical = 0.dp` margin to prevent double-gap compounding.<br>• Verify minimum card touch target height satisfies WCAG accessibility (>= 48.dp).<br>2. *Adaptive Compact Presentation Test (`EquipmentCompactCardTest.kt`)*:<br>• Verify 0-workout items (`totalWorkouts == 0`) resolve compact typography (`titleMedium`) and vertical padding (`8.dp`).<br>• Verify active items with workouts (`totalWorkouts > 0`) retain standard typography (`titleLarge`), 12.dp padding, and stats summary block.<br>• Verify retired items (`isRetired == true`) apply `0.75f` alpha to `MappableListItem`.<br>3. *Interactive Invariants Test*:<br>• Verify single-tap triggers `onConfigClick`.<br>• Verify long-press triggers delete dropdown menu anchored at `TopStart` per `REQ-UI-061`.<br>4. *Clean-Room Regression Execution*:<br>• Run `./gradlew testDebugUnitTest` verifying 100% pass rate. | Equipment list items exhibit clean 6dp–8dp inter-card spacing without 20dp compounding gaps; inactive and 0-workout items present compact card layout while preserving 48dp minimum touch target and delete contract; 100% test pass rate. | Verified |

| **TST-UI-218** | `ATT-2057` | **Conditional Research Button Visibility Based on Real Paired Remote Devices Verification** | `REQ-UI-259` | 1. *ViewModel Reactive Paired Remote State Tests (`ControlTrackingViewModelTest.kt`)*:<br>• Verify `hasPairedRemoteDevices` emits `false` when list contains only `Protocol.SMARTPHONE` devices (e.g. GPS, Battery).<br>• Verify `hasPairedRemoteDevices` emits `true` when a paired `Protocol.BLUETOOTH_LE` or `Protocol.ANT_PLUS` device is present with `isPaired == true`.<br>• Verify `hasPairedRemoteDevices` emits `false` when a Bluetooth LE or ANT+ device exists with `isPaired == false`.<br>2. *Database Direct Fast Query Test (`DevicesDatabaseManagerTest.kt`)*:<br>• Verify `DevicesDatabaseManager.hasPairedRemoteDevices()` executes optimized `LIMIT 1` query filtering `paired > 0 AND (protocol = 'ANT_PLUS' OR protocol = 'BLUETOOTH_LE')`.<br>• Verify cursor closing and accurate boolean result on 0 vs 1 rows.<br>3. *UI Conditional Composition Contract Test (`ControlTrackingScreenTest.kt` / Previews)*:<br>• Verify `ControlTrackingScreen` renders `ResearchButton` when `showResearchButton == true` and hides it when `showResearchButton == false`.<br>• Verify central information area (`SearchArea` and `RemoteDevices`) preserves `Alignment.TopCenter` with zero horizontal layout shift.<br>4. *Brand Compliance & 9-Language Localization Audit*:<br>• Verify official ANT+ and Bluetooth trademark assets remain completely unmodified.<br>• Verify `R.string.research` ("Suchen") exists in all 9 supported locales (EN, DE, ES, FR, IT, JA, NL, PL, PT).<br>5. *Clean-Room Full Suite Regression Execution*:<br>• Run `./gradlew testDebugUnitTest` verifying 100% pass rate. | ResearchButton is conditionally rendered only when real paired remote devices exist in SQLite; cold-start instantaneous seeding prevents layout flicker; trademark logos unchanged; 100% unit test pass rate. | Verified |
| **TST-UI-219** | `ATT-2031` | **Centralized Zone Threshold Resolution & O(log N) Scrubbing Point Search Verification** | `REQ-UI-260` | 1. *Threshold Resolution Factory Unit Tests (`TelemetryZoneMathTest.kt`)*:<br>• Verify `TelemetryZoneMath.loadHeartRateThresholds` correctly queries `HR_BIKE` for `BSportType.BIKE` and `HR_RUN` for other sport types.<br>• Verify returning `null` if any zone threshold is $\le 0$.<br>• Verify `TelemetryZoneMath.loadPowerThresholds` queries `PWR_BIKE` and returns `PowerZoneThresholds` or `null` if invalid.<br>2. *Scrubbing Point Lookup Binary Search Unit Tests (`TelemetryMetricUtilsTest.kt`)*:<br>• Verify `TelemetryMetricUtils.findNearestPoint` on distance domain against standard `minByOrNull` across varying track lengths.<br>• Verify time domain lookup (`isTimeDomain = true`).<br>• Edge cases: Empty list returns `null`, single-element list returns that element, exact matches return matching point, points before start return first point, points after end return last point, intermediate values choose nearest neighbor.<br>3. *Component Call-Site DRY Verification*:<br>• Verify `ElevationProfile.kt`, `TelemetryMetricGraph.kt`, and `MapDetailLayout.kt` invoke `TelemetryZoneMath` and `TelemetryMetricUtils.findNearestPoint` without redundant boilerplate queries or linear scans.<br>4. *Clean-Room Full Suite Regression Execution*:<br>• Run `./gradlew testDebugUnitTest` verifying 100% pass rate. | Centralized zone resolution eliminates duplicate boilerplate; binary search resolves nearest scrubbing point in O(log N) comparisons with exact numerical parity to linear search; zero layout regressions; 100% unit test pass rate. | Verified |
| **TST-UI-220** | `ATT-2032` | **Canonical Horizontal Domain Evaluation & Total Span Engine (ProfileDomainMath) Verification** | `REQ-UI-261` | 1. *Pure Mathematical Unit Tests (`ProfileDomainMathTest.kt`)*:<br>• Verify `isTracklessWorkout` returns `true` for `(0.0, 100L)`, `false` for `(100.0, 100L)`, `false` for `(0.0, 0L)`, `false` for null/empty lists.<br>• Verify `isEffectiveTimeDomain` returns `true` for trackless sessions regardless of configured domain.<br>• Verify `isEffectiveTimeDomain` returns `true` when `domain == TIME` and `timeSec > 0`.\<br>• Verify `isEffectiveTimeDomain` falls back to `false` when `domain == TIME` but `timeSec <= 0` (corrupt track safety).<br>• Verify `calculateTotalSpan` accurately resolves seconds vs meters across combinations.<br>2. *Component Integration & DRY Verification*:<br>• Verify `ElevationProfile.kt`, `TelemetryMetricGraph.kt`, and `MapDetailLayout.kt` consume `ProfileDomainMath` exclusively.<br>• Verify `TelemetryMetricGraph.kt` eliminates `minByOrNull` cursor scan.<br>3. *9-Language Localization Audit (`TranslationParityTest.kt`)*:<br>• Verify 100% parity across all 9 localized `strings.xml` files.<br>4. *Clean-Room Full Suite Regression Execution*:<br>• Run `./gradlew testDebugUnitTest` verifying 100% pass rate. | ProfileDomainMath provides unified single source of truth for horizontal domain and span calculations; corrupt zero-timestamp tracks gracefully fall back to distance; stationary sessions resolve to time; 100% unit test pass rate. | Verified |

| **TST-UI-257** | `ATT-2512` | **Distance-Weighted Elevation Profile Gaussian Smoothing & Continuous Slope Grade Verification** | `REQ-UI-297` | 1. *Pure Kernel Mathematical Unit Tests (`ElevationSmoothingMathTest.kt`)*:<br>• Verify distance-weighted Gaussian smoothing on synthetic quantized step data (0.2m–0.4m steps over 20m plateaus) eliminates flat plateaus and yields smooth, monotonic slope transitions.<br>• Verify exact boundary weight renormalization: start boundary ($d=0$) and end boundary ($d=D_{total}$) altitudes match terrain endpoints within 0.1% without artificial sag or peak distortion.<br>• Verify true geographic features (crests, saddles, summits) are preserved within $\pm 0.5\text{ m}$ of true elevation.<br>• Verify degenerate input handling: empty list, single point, identical coordinates, non-positive distances return cleanly without NaN or exceptions.<br>2. *Complexity & Performance Benchmark Unit Test (`ElevationSmoothingMathTest.kt`)*:<br>• Verify that smoothing 10,000 dense points ($N = 10,000$) executes in $< 15\text{ ms}$ on JVM.<br>3. *UI & Slope Derivative Integration Contract Tests (`ElevationProfileSmoothingContractTest.kt`)*:<br>• Verify `ElevationProfile.kt` invokes `ElevationSmoothingMath.smoothAltitudes`.<br>• Verify calculated `grade` produces continuous values without zebra oscillations between 0% and 15% on linear climbing slopes.<br>4. *Full Clean-Room Regression Suite*:<br>• Execute `./gradlew testDebugUnitTest` asserting 100% test pass rate with 0 regressions. | Distance-weighted Gaussian kernel smoothing eliminates elevation quantization staircase; boundary weight renormalization prevents sag; 100% test pass rate. | Verified |

| **TST-UI-259** | `ATT-2510` | **Elevation Profile Slope Gradient Preservation and X-Axis Climb Span Highlight Verification** | `REQ-UI-299` | 1. *Elevation Profile Climb Span Contract Tests (`ElevationProfileClimbSpanContractTest.kt`)*:<br>• Verify `ElevationProfile.kt` does NOT render accented ridge strokes over the elevation curve (no `drawLine` over `(px1, py1)` to `(px2, py2)` with climb category color).<br>• Verify `ElevationProfile.kt` renders horizontal X-axis span bars for recognized cycling climbs along baseline (`y = height`).<br>• Verify start and end distance bounds match `climb.pathPoints.first().distance` and `climb.pathPoints.last().distance` mapped via `distanceToCanvasX`.<br>• Verify climb span bar uses `getClimbCategoryColors(climb.category).first` with `strokeWidth = 4.dp.toPx()` and `StrokeCap.Round`.<br>• Verify `ClimbCategory.UNCATEGORIZED` climbs are excluded from X-axis span highlights.<br>• Verify summit badges/pins remain intact above peak coordinates.<br>2. *Full Clean-Room Regression Suite*:<br>• Execute `./gradlew testDebugUnitTest` asserting 100% test pass rate with 0 regressions. | Elevation profile curve strictly preserves physical slope grade coloration; horizontal X-axis displays categorized climb span bars; 100% unit test pass rate. | Verified |

segments exceeding 25 m tolerance are rejected.<br>• Verify directional bearing alignment: forward traversal ($\Delta \theta \le 45^\circ$, dot product $> 0$) passes; reverse direction ($\Delta \theta > 90^\circ$, negative dot product) or crossing perpendicular corridors rejected.<br>• Verify progression and length consistency: $D_{end} > D_{start}$ strictly enforced, and route span distance approximates segment distance within tolerance ($|(D_{end} - D_{start}) - \text{segment.distance}| \le \max(100.0, \text{segment.distance} \times 0.25)$).<br>• Verify sorting: matched segments returned strictly sorted in ascending order of `startDistanceMeters`.<br>2. *Breakdown UI & Tab Coexistence Contract Tests (`RouteSegmentsBreakdownContractTest.kt`)*:<br>• Verify `RouteSegmentsBreakdownSection` renders `ElevatedCard` with sport icon, segment name, category badge, and athlete PR badge.<br>• Verify metrics row displays start kilometer along route, segment distance, and average gradient.<br>• Verify `RouteOnMapScreen` renders Material 3 `FilterChip` toggle row when both climbs and segments exist, persisting selection via `rememberSaveable`.<br>• Verify `RouteOnMapScreen` renders only `RouteClimbsBreakdownSection` when only climbs exist, and only `RouteSegmentsBreakdownSection` when only segments exist.<br>3. *9-Language Localization Parity Audit (`RouteSegmentsLocalizationTest.kt`)*:<br>• Verify `routes_segments_section_title`, `routes_segments_tab_title`, `routes_segment_start_at`, `routes_segment_pr` exist, are non-empty, and contain valid format specifiers across all 9 supported locales: `values/`, `values-de/`, `values-es/`, `values-fr/`, `values-it/`, `values-ja/`, `values-nl/`, `values-pl/`, `values-pt/`.<br>4. *Full Clean-Room Regression Suite*:<br>• Execute `./gradlew testDebugUnitTest` asserting 100% test pass rate with 0 regressions. | Spatial overlap matching engine reliably identifies forward-direction segments along route corridor; chronological breakdown section renders PR and route metrics; climbs/segments tab toggle operational; 100% 9-language localization parity; 100% unit test pass rate. | Verified |
